Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The lie_groups variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 630 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 237 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the lie_groups decomposition. By minimality, the element a^630 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 237-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
